# Skills · P · part 212

865 entries, protein-sequence-clustering-deduplication to protocol-wire. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **protein-sequence-clustering-deduplication**: Use when merging heterogeneous protein reference databases (e.g., CARD, NDARO, SARG, RefSeq homologs) and redundant near-identical sequences must be collapsed into a consistent type/subtype hierarchy before read mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-clustering-deduplication))
- **protein-sequence-conservation-quantification**: Use when a set of protein sequence variants (e.g. from a BLAST search or a UniProt/Ensembl variant download) has been aligned with MUSCLE and per-position absolute and percent amino acid occurrence matrices are needed downstream of or alongside 3D structure conservation mapping. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-conservation-quantification))
- **protein-sequence-data-processing**: Clean, validate, and preprocess protein-sequence datasets into model-ready tables. Called after profiling-datasets — receives a loaded DataFrame and Dataset Profile; does not re-detect format or reload data. ([AI-BIIE-Initiative/capo](https://github.com/AI-BIIE-Initiative/capo/tree/HEAD/skills/data-processing/protein-sequence-data) · ★1)
- **protein-sequence-deduplication**: Use when a positive/negative protein sequence dataset (e.g., R protein vs non-R protein FASTA) must be redundancy-reduced before feature embedding and classifier training, to prevent near-identical homologs from inflating cross-validation performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-deduplication))
- **protein-sequence-descriptor-extraction**: Use when you need per-descriptor protein feature matrices (.mat workspaces for DDE, PSSM-DBT, PSSM-DWT, NMBAC) from FASTA sequences plus PSI-BLAST PSSM profiles, e.g. for ablation or downstream SVM-RFE+CBR selection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-descriptor-extraction))
- **protein-sequence-dipeptide-feature-extraction**: Use when raw protein sequences (e.g. FASTA from PDB1075, PDB594, PDB186) must be converted into fixed-length dipeptide-composition feature vectors for machine-learning classification of DNA-binding proteins. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-dipeptide-feature-extraction))
- **protein-sequence-embedding**: Use when aligning genes across species (e.g. human vs mouse scRNA-seq) by protein sequence similarity and you need embedding-based gene matches or a gene distance matrix for TACTiCS, as an alternative to BLAST -hit matching. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/protein-sequence-embedding))
- **protein-sequence-embedding-generation**: Use when raw protein sequences in FASTA format must be converted into fixed-length numerical feature vectors for machine-learning classification, following the deep representation learning approach of prPred-DRLF. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-embedding-generation))
- **protein-sequence-extraction-from-genbank**: Use when you have antiSMASH/gutSMASH output directories in GenBank format and need the predicted gene-cluster protein sequences as input for MASH MinHash redundancy filtering or BiG-SCAPE GCF grouping in BiG-MAP. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/protein-sequence-extraction-from-genbank))
- **protein-sequence-fasta-batch-processing**: Use when a whole-proteome or large multi-protein FASTA (e.g. Z. mays, T. aestivum, A. sativa, H. vulgare) must be converted into per-residue plus protein-average ProtT5 embeddings as input to downstream phosphorylation site prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-fasta-batch-processing))
- **protein-sequence-feature-dimensionality-reduction**: Use when a high-dimensional concatenated protein-sequence feature vector (e.g. 1011-D word-embedding + BOW features) must be compressed to a compact representation (505-D) before training a tree-based classifier such as XGBoost. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-feature-dimensionality-reduction))
- **protein-sequence-feature-vectorization**: Use when raw protein sequences (PDB1075/PDB594/PDB186 style FASTA) must be turned into fixed-length numeric descriptors before machine-learning classification, and PSI-BLAST PSSM profiles plus physicochemical properties are available. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-feature-vectorization))
- **protein-sequence-fragmentation-into-words**: Use when raw FASTA protein sequences must be converted into token units for NLP-based feature extraction (CBOW word embedding or BOW wordbook features) prior to classifier training. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-fragmentation-into-words))
- **protein-sequence-function**: Use for PaddleHelix protein sequence pretraining, prediction, protein function workflows, tokenizer/model guidance, and safe protein input validation. ([VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill](https://github.com/VectorSpaceLab/AREX-Skill/tree/HEAD/skills/repositories/repo-skills/paddlehelix/sub-skills/protein-sequence-function) · ★357 · 2 copies)
- **protein-sequence-gene-clustering**: Use when you have protein FASTA files (*.faa) for thousands of genomes (complete or MAGs/SAGs of mixed quality) and need gene clusters to feed a core/accessory genome analysis or other trait presence/absence computation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-gene-clustering))
- **protein-sequence-homology-blast-verification**: Use when validating that deduced proteins from an assembled transcriptome (e.g. GAWN/TransDecoder prot.fa outputs) match known homologs, or when confirming identity of a reconstructed protein like chicken SSPO against AJ866919. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-homology-blast-verification))
- **protein-sequence-kmer-vectorization**: Use when raw protein FASTA sequences must be converted to fixed-length feature representations for classification, clustering, or search, and you want to test whether amino acid recoding plus k-mering outperforms naive unencoded k-mer features. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-kmer-vectorization))
- **protein-sequence-loading-from-fasta**: Use when protein sequences live in a fasta file (e.g. HUMAN_2015_02_selected.fasta from UniProt 2015_02) and peptide identifications from an mzIdentML file (e.g. Thermo_Hela_PRTC_selected.mzid) must be combined into Proteins objects for plotting. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-loading-from-fasta))
- **protein-sequence-logo-generation**: Use when a FASTA file of protein sequence variants (or a MUSCLE alignment) has been analyzed for conservation and a visual, per-position residue-frequency representation is required. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/protein-sequence-logo-generation))
- **protein-sequence-msa**: Performs multiple sequence alignment of proteins with EBI Clustal Omega. Use when you need to align multiple sequences to assess similarity, domain conservation, or key residue conservation. Supports up to 4000 sequences and a maximum file size of 4 MB. Do not use to search for homologous proteins in a database (use MMseqs2, BLAST), align non-protein sequences (DNA, RNA), perform structural alignment (use Foldseek, PyMOL), or if you only have a single sequence. ([google-deepmind/science-skills](https://github.com/google-deepmind/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/protein_sequence_msa) · ★3.5k · 77 copies)
- **protein-sequence-msa**: Source: `science_skills/protein_sequence_msa/` ([Ryukijano/agent-skills](https://github.com/Ryukijano/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/.cursor/skills/protein-sequence-msa) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **protein-sequence-msa**: Align protein sequences with EBI Clustal Omega (up to 4000 sequences, 4 MB): assess similarity, domain and residue conservation. Not for searching homologues (MMseqs2/BLAST), DNA/RNA, structural alignment (Foldseek/PyMOL) or a single sequence. - **protein-sequence-prediction-benchmarking**: Use when a new sequence-classification predictor must be compared to prior tools (e.g., prPred, HMMER) on the same labeled FASTA test set to quantify ACC/F1/AUC/recall improvements. - **protein-sequence-similarity-search**: Searches for homologous protein sequences using MMseqs2 (fast, default) or BLAST (comprehensive, fallback). Trigger this whenever the user provides a protein sequence or FASTA file and asks to find homologues, sequence matches, or wants to infer protein function based on sequence similarity, but not when the user wants to infer protein function based on structural similarity. ([google-deepmind/science-skills](https://github.com/google-deepmind/science-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/protein_sequence_similarity_search) · ★3.5k · 78 copies)

- **protein-similarity-search**: Find homologous protein sequences with MMseqs2 (fast, default) or BLAST (comprehensive fallback) from a sequence or FASTA. Use to find sequence homologs or infer function by sequence similarity — not by structural similarity. Public APIs, no credential needed. ([eightmm/codex-science](https://github.com/eightmm/codex-science/tree/HEAD/authored-skills/protein-similarity-search) · ★4)
